const VERSION: &str = env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION");

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
struct Theme {
    enabled: bool,
}
impl Theme {
    fn new() -> Self {
        let enabled = env::var_os("NO_COLOR").is_none() && io::stderr().is_terminal();
        Self { enabled }
    }
    fn paint(self, code: &'static str, text: impl AsRef<str>) -> String {
        if self.enabled {
            format!("{code}{}{RESET}", text.as_ref())
        } else {
            text.as_ref().to_string()
        }
    }
}

const RESET: &str = "\x1b[0m";
const DIM: &str = "\x1b[2m";
const BOLD: &str = "\x1b[1m";
const RED: &str = "\x1b[31m";
const GREEN: &str = "\x1b[32m";
const CYAN: &str = "\x1b[36m";
const BLUE: &str = "\x1b[34m";

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct Cli {
    input: PathBuf,
    output: Option<PathBuf>,
    compile: bool,
    target: String,
    backend: Option<String>,
    linker: Option<String>,
    cflags: Vec<String>,
    link_args: Vec<String>,
    defines: Vec<String>,
    includes: Vec<PathBuf>,
    lib_paths: Vec<PathBuf>,
    libs: Vec<String>,
    verbose: bool,
    quiet: bool,
    dry_run: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum OutputKind {
    C,
    Lir,
    Binary,
}

fn usage() -> &'static str {
    r#"MAK — low-level compiler driver

USAGE
    mak [OPTIONS] <INPUT.mak>

OUTPUT
    -o, --output <PATH>       Output artifact / binary name.
                              No -cc: writes PATH.c unless PATH is .c/.mLIR/.LIR.
                              With -cc: PATH is the binary; PATH.c is kept beside it.
                              .LIR/.lir/.mlir are normalized to .mLIR and emit MAK LIR.

COMPILATION
    -cc, --compile            Generate C and invoke the backend compiler.
    -bcc=<NAME>               Backend compiler: clang, gcc, cc, zig. `zig` means `zig cc`.
        --backend <NAME>      Same as -bcc=NAME.
    -t, --target <TARGET>     Target name or triple. `native` uses the host.
                              Easy names: x64, arm64, x86, win64, arm, riscv64.

LINKING / C
    --linker <NAME>           Linker passed through -fuse-ld=<NAME> (mold, lld, ...).
    -C, --cflag <FLAG>        Extra C compiler flag; repeatable.
    --link-arg <ARG>          Extra linker argument; repeatable.
    -D, --define <NAME[=V]>   Add a C preprocessor definition.
    -I, --include <DIR>       Add a header search directory.
    -L, --lib-path <DIR>      Add a library search directory.
    -l, --lib <NAME>          Link a library.

DRIVER
    -v, --verbose             Print compiler phases and the exact backend command.
    -q, --quiet               Suppress normal success messages.
        --dry-run             Do everything except invoke the backend compiler.
    -h, --help                Show this help.
    -V, --version             Show MAK version.

EXAMPLES
    mak main.mak
        → main.c

    mak main.mak -o game
        → game.c

    mak main.mak -cc -o game
        → game.c + game

    mak main.mak -cc -o game -t x86_64-linux-gnu -bcc=clang
        → cross-compiled game

    mak main.mak -cc -o game -bcc=zig -t x64 -ld=mold
        → Zig C driver + mold

    mak main.mak -o optimized.mLIR
        → optimized MAK LIR
"#
}

fn print_banner(theme: Theme) {
    let title = theme.paint(BOLD, "MAK");
    let sub = theme.paint(DIM, "low-level compiler driver");
    eprintln!("{}  {}", title, sub);
}

fn info(theme: Theme, msg: impl AsRef<str>) {
    eprintln!("{} {}", theme.paint(CYAN, "→"), msg.as_ref());
}

fn ok(theme: Theme, msg: impl AsRef<str>) {
    eprintln!("{} {}", theme.paint(GREEN, "✓"), msg.as_ref());
}

fn fmt_duration(d: Duration) -> String {
    if d.as_secs() > 0 {
        format!("{:.2}s", d.as_secs_f64())
    } else if d.as_millis() > 0 {
        format!("{}ms", d.as_millis())
    } else {
        format!("{}µs", d.as_micros())
    }
}

fn line_col(src: &[u8], offset: u32) -> (u32, u32) {
    let off = (offset as usize).min(src.len());
    let (mut line, mut col) = (1u32, 1u32);
    for &b in &src[..off] {
        if b == b'\n' {
            line += 1;
            col = 1;
        } else {
            col += 1;
        }
    }
    (line, col)
}

fn get_line(src: &[u8], line: u32) -> (usize, usize) {
    let (mut n, mut start) = (1u32, 0usize);
    for (i, &b) in src.iter().enumerate() {
        if n == line {
            if b == b'\n' {
                return (start, i);
            }
        } else if b == b'\n' {
            n += 1;
            start = i + 1;
        }
    }
    if n == line {
        (start, src.len())
    } else {
        (0, 0)
    }
}

fn print_error(theme: Theme, path: &str, src: &[u8], span: Span, msg: &str, note: Option<&str>) {
    let (line, col) = line_col(src, span.lo);
    let (ls, le) = get_line(src, line);
    let line_bytes = &src[ls..le.min(src.len())];
    let line_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(line_bytes);

    let mut caret = String::new();
    for i in 0..(col as usize).saturating_sub(1) {
        caret.push(if line_bytes.get(i).copied() == Some(b'\t') {
            '\t'
        } else {
            ' '
        });
    }
    let width = (span.hi.saturating_sub(span.lo)).max(1) as usize;
    caret.extend(std::iter::repeat('^').take(width));

    let line_num = line.to_string();
    let pad = " ".repeat(line_num.len());
    let loc = format!("{path}:{line}:{col}");

    eprintln!("{}: {}", theme.paint(RED, "error"), theme.paint(BOLD, msg));
    eprintln!(" {} {}", theme.paint(BLUE, "-->"), theme.paint(CYAN, loc));
    eprintln!(
        " {}{}{}",
        theme.paint(BLUE, pad.as_str()),
        theme.paint(BLUE, " |"),
        ""
    );
    eprintln!(
        " {}{} {}",
        theme.paint(BLUE, &format!("{line_num:>w$}", w = line_num.len())),
        theme.paint(BLUE, " |"),
        line_str
    );
    eprintln!(
        " {}{} {}",
        theme.paint(BLUE, pad.as_str()),
        theme.paint(BLUE, " |"),
        theme.paint(RED, caret)
    );
    eprintln!(
        " {}{} {}",
        theme.paint(BLUE, pad.as_str()),
        theme.paint(BLUE, " |"),
        theme.paint(DIM, format!("span {}..{}", span.lo, span.hi))
    );
    if let Some(note) = note {
        eprintln!("  {} {}", theme.paint(CYAN, "note:"), note);
    }
    eprintln!();
}

fn parse_cli(theme: Theme) -> Result<Cli, String> {
    let args = env::args().skip(1).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--help" || a == "-h") {
        println!("{}", usage());
        std::process::exit(0);
    }
    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--version" || a == "-V") {
        println!("mak {VERSION}");
        std::process::exit(0);
    }

    if args.is_empty() {
        return Err("no input file supplied\n\nRun `mak --help` for usage.".into());
    }

    let mut input = None;
    let mut output: Option<PathBuf> = None;
    let mut compile = false;
    let mut target = "native".to_string();
    let mut backend = None;
    let mut linker = None;
    let mut cflags = Vec::new();
    let mut link_args = Vec::new();
    let mut defines = Vec::new();
    let mut includes = Vec::new();
    let mut lib_paths = Vec::new();
    let mut libs = Vec::new();
    let mut verbose = false;
    let mut quiet = false;
    let mut dry_run = false;

    let mut i = 0;
    while i < args.len() {
        let raw = args[i].clone();
        if raw == "--" {
            i += 1;
            if i < args.len() {
                if input.is_some() {
                    return Err("multiple input files are not supported yet".into());
                }
                input = Some(PathBuf::from(&args[i]));
                i += 1;
            }
            if i != args.len() {
                return Err("unexpected arguments after input file".into());
            }
            break;
        }

        match raw.as_str() {
            "-cc" | "--compile" => compile = true,
            "-v" | "--verbose" => verbose = true,
            "-q" | "--quiet" => quiet = true,
            "--dry-run" => dry_run = true,
            "-o" | "--output" => {
                i += 1;
                output = Some(PathBuf::from(required(&args, i, "-o/--output")?));
            }
            "-t" | "--target" => {
                i += 1;
                target = required(&args, i, "-t/--target")?;
            }
            "--backend" | "-bcc" => {
                i += 1;
                backend = Some(required(&args, i, "--backend/-bcc")?);
            }
            "--linker" | "-ld" | "-lld" | "-ldd" => {
                i += 1;
                linker = Some(required(&args, i, "--linker/-ld")?);
            }
            "-C" | "--cflag" => {
                i += 1;
                cflags.push(required(&args, i, "-C/--cflag")?);
            }
            "--link-arg" => {
                i += 1;
                link_args.push(required(&args, i, "--link-arg")?);
            }
            "-D" | "--define" => {
                i += 1;
                defines.push(required(&args, i, "-D/--define")?);
            }
            "-I" | "--include" => {
                i += 1;
                includes.push(PathBuf::from(required(&args, i, "-I/--include")?));
            }
            "-L" | "--lib-path" => {
                i += 1;
                lib_paths.push(PathBuf::from(required(&args, i, "-L/--lib-path")?));
            }
            "-l" | "--lib" => {
                i += 1;
                libs.push(required(&args, i, "-l/--lib")?);
            }
            _ if raw.starts_with("-o=") || raw.starts_with("--output=") => {
                output = Some(PathBuf::from(raw.split_once('=').unwrap().1));
            }
            _ if raw.starts_with("-t=") || raw.starts_with("--target=") => {
                target = raw.split_once('=').unwrap().1.to_string();
            }
            _ if raw.starts_with("-bcc=") || raw.starts_with("--backend=") => {
                backend = Some(raw.split_once('=').unwrap().1.to_string());
            }
            _ if raw.starts_with("-ld=")
                || raw.starts_with("--linker=")
                || raw.starts_with("-lld=")
                || raw.starts_with("-ldd=") =>
            {
                linker = Some(raw.split_once('=').unwrap().1.to_string());
            }
            _ if raw.starts_with("-C=") || raw.starts_with("--cflag=") => {
                cflags.push(raw.split_once('=').unwrap().1.to_string());
            }
            _ if raw.starts_with("--link-arg=") => {
                link_args.push(raw.split_once('=').unwrap().1.to_string());
            }
            _ if raw.starts_with("-D=") || raw.starts_with("--define=") => {
                defines.push(raw.split_once('=').unwrap().1.to_string());
            }
            _ if raw.starts_with("-D") && raw.len() > 2 => defines.push(raw[2..].to_string()),
            _ if raw.starts_with("-I") && raw.len() > 2 => includes.push(PathBuf::from(&raw[2..])),
            _ if raw.starts_with("-L") && raw.len() > 2 => lib_paths.push(PathBuf::from(&raw[2..])),
            _ if raw.starts_with("-l") && raw.len() > 2 => libs.push(raw[2..].to_string()),
            _ if raw.starts_with('-') => {
                return Err(format!("unknown option `{raw}`"));
            }
            _ => {
                if input.is_some() {
                    return Err("multiple input files are not supported yet".into());
                }
                input = Some(PathBuf::from(args[i].clone()));
            }
        }
        i += 1;
    }

    let input = input.ok_or_else(|| "no input file supplied".to_string())?;
    if target.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err("target cannot be empty".into());
    }
    if linker.as_deref() == Some("") {
        linker = None;
    }
    Ok(Cli {
        input,
        output,
        compile,
        target,
        backend,
        linker,
        cflags,
        link_args,
        defines,
        includes,
        lib_paths,
        libs,
        verbose,
        quiet,
        dry_run,
    })
}

fn required(args: &[String], index: usize, flag: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    args.get(index)
        .cloned()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{flag} expects a value"))
}

fn target_alias(input: &str) -> String {
    let t = input.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
    match t.as_str() {
        "native" | "host" | "local" => "native".into(),
        "x64" | "amd64" | "x86_64" | "linux-x64" | "linux-amd64" | "x64-linux" | "x86_64-linux"
        | "x64-linux-gnu" => "x86_64-linux-gnu".into(),
        "arm64" | "aarch64" | "linux-arm64" | "arm64-linux" | "aarch64-linux"
        | "arm64-linux-gnu" => "aarch64-linux-gnu".into(),
        "win64" | "windows-x64" | "windows-amd64" | "x64-windows" | "x86_64-windows"
        | "x64-windows-gnu" => "x86_64-windows-gnu".into(),
        "x86" | "i386" | "i686" | "linux-x86" | "x86-linux" | "i686-linux" => {
            "i686-linux-gnu".into()
        }
        "arm" | "arm32" | "armv7" | "armv7-linux" => "armv7-linux-gnueabihf".into(),
        "riscv64" | "rv64" | "riscv64-linux" => "riscv64-linux-gnu".into(),
        other => other.to_string(),
    }
}

fn output_kind(path: &Path, compile: bool, explicit: bool) -> OutputKind {
    let ext = path
        .extension()
        .and_then(|x| x.to_str())
        .unwrap_or("")
        .to_ascii_lowercase();

    if ext == "mlir" || ext == "lir" {
        OutputKind::Lir
    } else if ext == "c" {
        OutputKind::C
    } else if compile {
        OutputKind::Binary
    } else if explicit {
        OutputKind::C
    } else {
        OutputKind::C
    }
}

fn normalize_lir_path(mut path: PathBuf) -> PathBuf {
    path.set_extension("mLIR");
    path
}

fn default_base(input: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    input.with_extension("")
}

fn derive_c_path(output: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    output.with_extension("c")
}

fn derive_binary_from_c(c_path: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    c_path.with_extension("")
}

fn ensure_parent(path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        if !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() && parent != Path::new(".") {
            fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn command_in_path(program: &str) -> bool {
    let p = Path::new(program);
    if p.components().count() > 1 {
        return p.is_file();
    }
    let Some(path) = env::var_os("PATH") else {
        return false;
    };
    env::split_paths(&path).any(|dir| dir.join(program).is_file())
}

fn shell_split(s: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let mut cur = String::new();
    let mut quote = None;
    let mut escaped = false;
    let mut started = false;

    for ch in s.chars() {
        if escaped {
            cur.push(ch);
            escaped = false;
            started = true;
            continue;
        }
        match quote {
            Some(q) if ch == q => quote = None,
            Some(_) => cur.push(ch),
            None if ch == '\\' => escaped = true,
            None if ch == '\'' || ch == '"' => {
                quote = Some(ch);
                started = true;
            }
            None if ch.is_whitespace() => {
                if started {
                    out.push(std::mem::take(&mut cur));
                    started = false;
                }
            }
            None => {
                cur.push(ch);
                started = true;
            }
        }
    }
    if escaped {
        return Err("unterminated escape in backend command".into());
    }
    if quote.is_some() {
        return Err("unterminated quote in backend command".into());
    }
    if started {
        out.push(cur);
    }
    if out.is_empty() {
        return Err("backend command cannot be empty".into());
    }
    Ok(out)
}

fn default_backend() -> String {
    if command_in_path("clang") {
        "clang".into()
    } else if command_in_path("gcc") {
        "gcc".into()
    } else if command_in_path("cc") {
        "cc".into()
    } else if command_in_path("zig") {
        "zig".into()
    } else {
        "cc".into()
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum BackendFamily {
    Clang,
    Gcc,
    Zig,
    Unknown,
}

fn backend_family(parts: &[String]) -> BackendFamily {
    if parts.iter().any(|p| {
        Path::new(p)
            .file_name()
            .and_then(|x| x.to_str())
            .unwrap_or(p)
            .eq_ignore_ascii_case("zig")
    }) {
        return BackendFamily::Zig;
    }
    for p in parts.iter().rev() {
        let n = Path::new(p)
            .file_name()
            .and_then(|x| x.to_str())
            .unwrap_or(p)
            .to_ascii_lowercase();
        if n == "clang" || n.starts_with("clang-") || n == "clang++" {
            return BackendFamily::Clang;
        }
        if n == "gcc" || n.starts_with("gcc-") || n.ends_with("-gcc") || n == "cc" {
            return BackendFamily::Gcc;
        }
    }
    BackendFamily::Unknown
}

fn find_gcc_cross(target: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let mut candidates = vec![format!("{target}-gcc")];
    if let Some(rest) = target.strip_prefix("x86_64-unknown-") {
        candidates.push(format!("x86_64-{rest}-gcc"));
    }
    if let Some(rest) = target.strip_prefix("aarch64-unknown-") {
        candidates.push(format!("aarch64-{rest}-gcc"));
    }
    candidates.into_iter().find(|x| command_in_path(x))
}

fn pretty_command(program: &str, args: &[String]) -> String {
    let mut out = quote_arg(program);
    for arg in args {
        out.push(' ');
        out.push_str(&quote_arg(arg));
    }
    out
}

fn quote_arg(s: &str) -> String {
    if s.chars()
        .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || "-_./:=+,@%".contains(c))
    {
        return s.to_string();
    }
    let escaped = s.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('"', "\\\"");
    format!("\"{escaped}\"")
}

fn build_backend_command(
    cli: &Cli,
    c_path: &Path,
    binary: &Path,
) -> Result<(String, Vec<String>), String> {
    let requested = cli.backend.clone().unwrap_or_else(default_backend);
    let mut parts = shell_split(&requested)?;
    if parts.len() == 1
        && Path::new(&parts[0])
            .file_name()
            .and_then(|x| x.to_str())
            .map_or(false, |x| x.eq_ignore_ascii_case("zig"))
    {
        parts.push("cc".into());
    }
    let family = backend_family(&parts);
    let target = target_alias(&cli.target);

    if matches!(target.as_str(), "native" | "host") {
        // no target flag
    } else {
        match family {
            BackendFamily::Clang => {
                parts.push(format!("--target={target}"));
            }
            BackendFamily::Zig => {
                parts.push("-target".into());
                parts.push(target.clone());
            }
            BackendFamily::Gcc => {
                if let Some(cross) = find_gcc_cross(&target) {
                    if parts.len() == 1 {
                        parts[0] = cross;
                    } else {
                        return Err(format!(
                            "GCC cross target `{target}` needs `{target}-gcc`. Try `-bcc=clang` or `-bcc=zig` for target-aware compilation."
                        ));
                    }
                } else {
                    return Err(format!(
                        "backend `{requested}` does not provide a portable `-t {target}` interface. \
                         Install `{target}-gcc`, use `-bcc=clang`, or use `-bcc=\"zig cc\"`."
                    ));
                }
            }
            BackendFamily::Unknown => {
                return Err(format!(
                    "cannot apply target `{target}` to backend `{requested}`. Use clang, gcc/cross-gcc, or zig."
                ));
            }
        }
    }

    parts.push("-std=c11".into());
    parts.push("-O3".into());
    parts.push("-DNDEBUG".into());

    if let Some(linker) = cli.linker.as_deref() {
        if linker.eq_ignore_ascii_case("lld")
            || linker.eq_ignore_ascii_case("mold")
            || linker.starts_with("ld")
            || linker.contains('/')
            || linker
                .chars()
                .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '-' || c == '_' || c == '.')
        {
            parts.push(format!("-fuse-ld={linker}"));
        }
    }

    for flag in &cli.cflags {
        parts.push(flag.clone());
    }
    for def in &cli.defines {
        parts.push(format!("-D{def}"));
    }
    for dir in &cli.includes {
        parts.push(format!("-I{}", dir.display()));
    }
    for dir in &cli.lib_paths {
        parts.push(format!("-L{}", dir.display()));
    }

    parts.push(c_path.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
    parts.push("-o".into());
    parts.push(binary.to_string_lossy().into_owned());

    for arg in &cli.link_args {
        parts.push(arg.clone());
    }
    for lib in &cli.libs {
        if lib.starts_with("-l") {
            parts.push(lib.clone());
        } else {
            parts.push(format!("-l{lib}"));
        }
    }

    Ok((parts.remove(0), parts))
}

fn execute_backend(theme: Theme, cli: &Cli, c_path: &Path, binary: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
    let (program, args) = build_backend_command(cli, c_path, binary)
        .map_err(|msg| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, msg))?;

    if cli.verbose {
        eprintln!(
            "{} {}",
            theme.paint(DIM, "backend:"),
            pretty_command(&program, &args)
        );
    }

    if cli.dry_run {
        ok(theme, format!("dry-run: backend not invoked"));
        return Ok(());
    }

    if !command_in_path(&program) {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::NotFound,
            format!(
                "backend `{program}` was not found in PATH\n  hint: install it or choose another backend with `-bcc=clang`, `-bcc=gcc`, or `-bcc=zig`"
            ),
        ));
    }

    let status = Command::new(&program)
        .args(&args)
        .stdin(Stdio::inherit())
        .stdout(Stdio::inherit())
        .stderr(Stdio::inherit())
        .status()?;

    if !status.success() {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::Other,
            format!("backend `{program}` failed with {status}"),
        ));
    }

    if !binary.is_file() {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::Other,
            format!(
                "backend exited successfully but did not produce `{}`",
                binary.display()
            ),
        ));
    }

    Ok(())
}

fn load_program(theme: Theme, path: &Path) -> io::Result<Program> {
    let mut visited = HashSet::new();
    let mut final_decls = Vec::new();
    let mut queue = VecDeque::new();

    let root_canon = path.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf());
    visited.insert(root_canon.clone());

    let src = fs::read(path)?;
    let mut src_buf = src;
    src_buf.push(0);
    src_buf.push(0);

    let p = parser::Parser::parse(&src_buf).map_err(|errs| {
        for e in &errs {
            print_error(
                theme,
                path.to_str().unwrap_or("<input>"),
                &src_buf,
                e.span,
                &e.msg,
                e.note.as_deref(),
            );
        }
        io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, "parse error")
    })?;

    let base_dir = path.parent().unwrap_or(Path::new(".")).to_path_buf();
    queue.push_back((p.decls, base_dir));
    let body = p.body;

    while let Some((decls, dir)) = queue.pop_front() {
        for d in decls {
            match d {
                Decl::Bring(paths) => {
                    for import in paths {
                        let raw = Path::new(&import);
                        let abs = if raw.is_absolute() {
                            raw.to_path_buf()
                        } else {
                            dir.join(raw)
                        };
                        let canon = abs.canonicalize().unwrap_or(abs);
                        if visited.insert(canon.clone()) {
                            let sub_src = fs::read(&canon)?;
                            let mut sub_src_buf = sub_src;
                            sub_src_buf.push(0);
                            sub_src_buf.push(0);

                            let sub_p = parser::Parser::parse(&sub_src_buf).map_err(|errs| {
                                for e in &errs {
                                    print_error(
                                        theme,
                                        canon.to_str().unwrap_or("<import>"),
                                        &sub_src_buf,
                                        e.span,
                                        &e.msg,
                                        e.note.as_deref(),
                                    );
                                }
                                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, "parse error")
                            })?;

                            let sub_dir = canon.parent().unwrap_or(Path::new(".")).to_path_buf();
                            queue.push_back((sub_p.decls, sub_dir));
                        }
                    }
                }
                other => final_decls.push(other),
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(Program {
        decls: final_decls,
        body,
    })
}

fn compile(cli: Cli) -> io::Result<()> {
    let theme = Theme::new();
    if !cli.quiet {
        print_banner(theme);
    }

    let total = Instant::now();
    let input = &cli.input;

    if !input.is_file() {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::NotFound,
            format!(
                "source file not found: {}\n  hint: pass a `.mak` source file, e.g. `mak main.mak`",
                input.display()
            ),
        ));
    }

    let target = target_alias(&cli.target);
    let explicit_output = cli.output.is_some();
    let mut output = match &cli.output {
        Some(p) => p.clone(),
        None if cli.compile => default_base(input),
        None => derive_c_path(input),
    };

    let kind = output_kind(&output, cli.compile, explicit_output);
    if kind == OutputKind::Lir && cli.compile {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "`-cc` cannot be combined with an LIR output; choose a binary output name instead",
        ));
    }
    if kind == OutputKind::Lir {
        output = normalize_lir_path(output);
    }

    let (c_path, binary_path) = if cli.compile {
        match kind {
            OutputKind::Lir => unreachable!(),
            OutputKind::C => (output.clone(), Some(derive_binary_from_c(&output))),
            OutputKind::Binary => (derive_c_path(&output), Some(output.clone())),
        }
    } else {
        match kind {
            OutputKind::Lir => (PathBuf::new(), None),
            OutputKind::C => (output.clone(), None),
            OutputKind::Binary => (derive_c_path(&output), None),
        }
    };

    if kind == OutputKind::Lir {
        ensure_parent(&output)?;
    } else {
        ensure_parent(&c_path)?;
        if let Some(binary) = &binary_path {
            ensure_parent(binary)?;
        }
    }

    let phase_start = Instant::now();
    info(theme, format!("load {}", input.display()));
    let prog = load_program(theme, input)?;
    let load_time = phase_start.elapsed();

    let sema_start = Instant::now();
    let main_src = fs::read(input).unwrap_or_default();
    let prog = match sema::analyze(prog) {
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(errs) => {
            for e in &errs {
                print_error(
                    theme,
                    &input.display().to_string(),
                    &main_src,
                    e.span,
                    &e.msg,
                    e.note.as_deref(),
                );
            }
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "semantic analysis failed",
            ));
        }
    };
    let sema_time = sema_start.elapsed();

    let opt_start = Instant::now();
    // println!("+=+=+++\nast being  sent to obfuscate:\n{:?}",&prog);

    let prog = obfuscate::run(prog);
    // println!("ast being sent  to cgen:\n{:?}\n+=+=+++\n",&prog);
    let opt_time = opt_start.elapsed();

    if cli.verbose {
        eprintln!(
            "{} load={} sema={} optimize={}",
            theme.paint(DIM, "timing:"),
            fmt_duration(load_time),
            fmt_duration(sema_time),
            fmt_duration(opt_time),
        );
    }

    if kind == OutputKind::Lir {
        let lir_start = Instant::now();
        let lir = cgen::generate_mlir(&prog);
        fs::write(&output, lir.as_bytes())?;
        let t = lir_start.elapsed();
        ok(
            theme,
            format!(
                "generated mLIR {} ({} bytes, {})",
                output.display(),
                lir.len(),
                fmt_duration(t)
            ),
        );
        return Ok(());
    }

    let c_start = Instant::now();
    let c = cgen::generate(&prog);
    fs::write(&c_path, c.as_bytes())?;
    let c_time = c_start.elapsed();
    ok(
        theme,
        format!(
            "generated C {} ({} bytes, {})",
            c_path.display(),
            c.len(),
            fmt_duration(c_time)
        ),
    );

    if !cli.compile {
        return Ok(());
    }

    let binary = binary_path.expect("compile mode always has a binary path");
    let cc_start = Instant::now();
    execute_backend(theme, &cli, &c_path, &binary)?;
    let cc_time = cc_start.elapsed();

    if cli.dry_run {
        return Ok(());
    }

    ok(
        theme,
        format!(
            "built {} via {} ({}; target={})",
            binary.display(),
            cli.backend.as_deref().unwrap_or("auto"),
            fmt_duration(cc_time),
            target
        ),
    );

    if cli.verbose {
        let total_time = total.elapsed();
        eprintln!(
            "{} total={} source={} target={} backend={}",
            theme.paint(DIM, "summary:"),
            fmt_duration(total_time),
            input.display(),
            target,
            cli.backend.as_deref().unwrap_or("auto"),
        );
    }

    Ok(())
}

fn main() {
    let theme = Theme::new();
    match parse_cli(theme) {
        Ok(cli) => {
            if let Err(err) = compile(cli) {
                eprintln!("{}: {}", theme.paint(RED, "error"), err);
                std::process::exit(1);
            }
        }
        Err(msg) => {
            eprintln!("{}: {}\n\n{}", theme.paint(RED, "error"), msg, usage());
            std::process::exit(2);
        }
    }
}

#![allow(unused_variables, unused_mut, unreachable_patterns, dead_code)]

use crate::ast::*;
use crate::lexer::Span;
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

const NULL_SPAN: Span = Span { lo: 0, hi: 0 };
const LOCAL_LAYERS: usize = 128;

macro_rules! opt_debug {
    ($($arg:tt)*) => {{
        #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
        {
            eprintln!("  [opt-debug] {}", format_args!($($arg)*));
        }
    }};
}

macro_rules! opt_debug_delta {
    ($label:expr, $before:expr, $after:expr) => {{
        #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
        {
            debug_delta($label, $before, $after);
        }
    }};
}

#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
fn debug_delta(label: &str, before: &Program, after: &Program) {
    let b = measure(before);
    let a = measure(after);
    let bc = wpo_cost(before);
    let ac = wpo_cost(after);
    let dn = a.nodes as isize - b.nodes as isize;
    let df = a.fns as isize - b.fns as isize;
    let db = a.binders as isize - b.binders as isize;
    let dc = ac as isize - bc as isize;
    eprintln!(
        "  [opt-debug] {} candidate shape: nodes {} -> {} ({:+}), fns {} -> {} ({:+}), binders {} -> {} ({:+}), cost {} -> {} ({:+}, {:+.2}%), hash {:016x} -> {:016x}",
        label,
        b.nodes,
        a.nodes,
        dn,
        b.fns,
        a.fns,
        df,
        b.binders,
        a.binders,
        db,
        bc,
        ac,
        dc,
        pct_delta(bc, ac),
        fingerprint(before),
        fingerprint(after),
    );

    let names_before = before
        .decls
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|d| match d {
            Decl::Let { name, .. } => Some(name.as_str()),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect::<HashSet<_>>();
    let names_after = after
        .decls
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|d| match d {
            Decl::Let { name, .. } => Some(name.as_str()),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect::<HashSet<_>>();

    let mut removed = names_before
        .difference(&names_after)
        .copied()
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut added = names_after
        .difference(&names_before)
        .copied()
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    removed.sort_unstable();
    added.sort_unstable();

    if removed.is_empty() && added.is_empty() {
        eprintln!(
            "  [opt-debug] {} declarations: no top-level function/value names added or removed",
            label
        );
    } else {
        eprintln!(
            "  [opt-debug] {} declarations: -[{}] +[{}]",
            label,
            removed.join(", "),
            added.join(", "),
        );
    }
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct OptStat {
    hits: usize,
    delta: isize,
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct OptStats {
    rounds: usize,
    restarts: usize,
    passes: HashMap<String, OptStat>,
    rejected: usize,
    quarantined: HashSet<String>,
}

#[derive(Default, Clone, Copy)]
struct Measure {
    nodes: usize,
    fns: usize,
    binders: usize,
}

fn measure(p: &Program) -> Measure {
    fn pat(p: &Pat, m: &mut Measure) {
        match p {
            Pat::Bind(_) => m.binders += 1,
            Pat::Ctor { args, .. } => {
                for a in args {
                    pat(a, m);
                }
            }
            Pat::Tuple(xs) => {
                for x in xs {
                    pat(x, m);
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    fn expr(e: &Expr, m: &mut Measure) {
        m.nodes += 1;
        match &e.kind {
            ExprKind::Let { value, body, .. } => {
                m.binders += 1;
                expr(value, m);
                expr(body, m);
            }
            ExprKind::Fn { params, body } => {
                m.fns += 1;
                m.binders += params.len();
                expr(body, m);
            }
            ExprKind::App { fun, args } => {
                expr(fun, m);
                for a in args {
                    expr(a, m);
                }
            }
            ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } => {
                expr(scrut, m);
                for arm in arms {
                    pat(&arm.pat, m);
                    expr(&arm.body, m);
                }
            }
            ExprKind::Block { stmts, tail } => {
                for s in stmts {
                    expr(s, m);
                }
                expr(tail, m);
            }
            ExprKind::Bin { l, r, .. } => {
                expr(l, m);
                expr(r, m);
            }
            ExprKind::Un { e, .. } | ExprKind::Quote(e) => expr(e, m),
            ExprKind::Int(_) | ExprKind::Float(_) | ExprKind::Str(_) | ExprKind::Var(_) => {}
        }
    }

    let mut m = Measure::default();
    for d in &p.decls {
        m.nodes += 1;
        match d {
            Decl::Let { value, .. } => expr(value, &mut m),
            Decl::External(_) | Decl::Type { .. } | Decl::Bring(_) => {}
        }
    }
    expr(&p.body, &mut m);
    m
}

fn pct_delta(before: usize, after: usize) -> f64 {
    if before == 0 {
        0.0
    } else {
        ((after as f64 - before as f64) * 100.0) / before as f64
    }
}

fn print_pass_stats(s: &OptStats) {
    let order = [
        "beta",
        "known-case",
        "tree",
        "const",
        "global-const",
        "global-sccp",
        "param-drop",
        "inline",
        "mega",
        "advanced",
        "jump-thread",
        "sink",
        "word-sccp",
        "dce",
        "whole-dce",
    ];
    let mut parts = Vec::new();
    for name in order {
        if let Some(v) = s.passes.get(name) {
            if v.hits != 0 {
                let n = if v.delta >= 0 {
                    format!("-{} nodes", v.delta)
                } else {
                    format!("+{} nodes", -v.delta)
                };
                parts.push(format!("{}={} ({})", name, v.hits, n));
            }
        }
    }
    if parts.is_empty() {
        eprintln!("  passes: no effective rewrites");
    } else {
        eprintln!("  passes: {}", parts.join("  "));
    }
    if s.rejected != 0 {
        eprintln!("  safety guards: {} rejected", s.rejected);
    }
    if !s.quarantined.is_empty() {
        let mut q = s.quarantined.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        q.sort_unstable();
        eprintln!("  quarantined: {}", q.join(", "));
    }
}

const WPO_ROUNDS: usize = 20;
const WPO_MAX_COMMITS: usize = 160;
const WPO_COST_GROWTH_NUM: usize = 3;
const WPO_COST_GROWTH_DEN: usize = 2;

#[derive(Default)]
struct WpoStats {
    rounds: usize,
    commits: usize,
    rejected: usize,
    const_folds: usize,
    phase_hits: HashMap<String, usize>,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
struct WpoSummary {
    functions: HashMap<String, (Vec<String>, Expr)>,
    graph: HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>,
    recursive: HashSet<String>,
    pure: HashSet<String>,
    calls: HashMap<String, usize>,
    sizes: HashMap<String, usize>,
}

fn wpo_function_map(p: &Program) -> HashMap<String, (Vec<String>, Expr)> {
    let mut out = HashMap::new();
    for d in &p.decls {
        if let Decl::Let { name, value, .. } = d {
            if let ExprKind::Fn { params, body } = &value.kind {
                out.insert(name.clone(), (params.clone(), (**body).clone()));
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

fn wpo_graph_and_calls(
    p: &Program,
    funcs: &HashMap<String, (Vec<String>, Expr)>,
) -> (HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>, HashMap<String, usize>) {
    let names: HashSet<String> = funcs.keys().cloned().collect();
    let mut graph = HashMap::<String, HashSet<String>>::new();
    let mut calls_total = HashMap::<String, usize>::new();

    fn count_expr(
        e: &Expr,
        names: &HashSet<String>,
        graph_out: &mut HashSet<String>,
        total: &mut HashMap<String, usize>,
    ) {
        match &e.kind {
            ExprKind::App { fun, args } => {
                if let ExprKind::Var(n) = &fun.kind {
                    if names.contains(n) {
                        graph_out.insert(n.clone());
                        *total.entry(n.clone()).or_default() += 1;
                    }
                }
                count_expr(fun, names, graph_out, total);
                for a in args {
                    count_expr(a, names, graph_out, total);
                }
            }
            ExprKind::Let { value, body, .. } => {
                count_expr(value, names, graph_out, total);
                count_expr(body, names, graph_out, total);
            }
            ExprKind::Fn { body, .. } => count_expr(body, names, graph_out, total),
            ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } => {
                count_expr(scrut, names, graph_out, total);
                for a in arms {
                    count_expr(&a.body, names, graph_out, total);
                }
            }
            ExprKind::Block { stmts, tail } => {
                for s in stmts {
                    count_expr(s, names, graph_out, total);
                }
                count_expr(tail, names, graph_out, total);
            }
            ExprKind::Bin { l, r, .. } => {
                count_expr(l, names, graph_out, total);
                count_expr(r, names, graph_out, total);
            }
            ExprKind::Un { e, .. } | ExprKind::Quote(e) => count_expr(e, names, graph_out, total),
            ExprKind::Int(_) | ExprKind::Float(_) | ExprKind::Str(_) | ExprKind::Var(_) => {}
        }
    }

    for (name, (_, body)) in funcs {
        let mut edges = HashSet::new();
        count_expr(body, &names, &mut edges, &mut calls_total);
        graph.insert(name.clone(), edges);
    }

    let mut body_edges = HashSet::new();
    count_expr(&p.body, &names, &mut body_edges, &mut calls_total);
    let _ = body_edges;
    (graph, calls_total)
}

fn wpo_sccs(graph: &HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>) -> Vec<Vec<String>> {
    fn dfs(
        v: &str,
        graph: &HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>,
        seen: &mut HashSet<String>,
        order: &mut Vec<String>,
    ) {
        if !seen.insert(v.to_string()) {
            return;
        }
        if let Some(next) = graph.get(v) {
            for n in next {
                dfs(n, graph, seen, order);
            }
        }
        order.push(v.to_string());
    }

    fn rev_graph(graph: &HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>) -> HashMap<String, HashSet<String>> {
        let mut rev = HashMap::<String, HashSet<String>>::new();
        for n in graph.keys() {
            rev.entry(n.clone()).or_default();
        }
        for (a, bs) in graph {
            for b in bs {
                rev.entry(b.clone()).or_default().insert(a.clone());
            }
        }
        rev
    }

    fn collect(
        v: &str,
        graph: &HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>,
        seen: &mut HashSet<String>,
        comp: &mut Vec<String>,
    ) {
        if !seen.insert(v.to_string()) {
            return;
        }
        comp.push(v.to_string());
        if let Some(next) = graph.get(v) {
            for n in next {
                collect(n, graph, seen, comp);
            }
        }
    }

    let mut seen = HashSet::new();
    let mut order = Vec::with_capacity(graph.len());
    for n in graph.keys() {
        dfs(n, graph, &mut seen, &mut order);
    }

    let rev = rev_graph(graph);
    let mut seen_rev = HashSet::new();
    let mut sccs = Vec::new();
    while let Some(v) = order.pop() {
        let mut comp = Vec::new();
        collect(&v, &rev, &mut seen_rev, &mut comp);
        if !comp.is_empty() {
            sccs.push(comp);
        }
    }
    sccs
}

fn wpo_recursive_from_graph(graph: &HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>) -> HashSet<String> {
    let mut out = HashSet::new();
    for comp in wpo_sccs(graph) {
        if comp.len() > 1 {
            out.extend(comp);
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(n) = comp.first() {
            if graph.get(n).map_or(false, |xs| xs.contains(n)) {
                out.insert(n.clone());
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

fn wpo_pure_expr(e: &Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, local_scc: &HashSet<String>) -> bool {
    match &e.kind {
        ExprKind::Int(_) | ExprKind::Float(_) | ExprKind::Str(_) | ExprKind::Var(_) => true,
        ExprKind::Fn { body, .. } => wpo_pure_expr(body, pure, local_scc),
        ExprKind::Quote(_) => true,
        ExprKind::Let { value, body, .. } => {
            wpo_pure_expr(value, pure, local_scc) && wpo_pure_expr(body, pure, local_scc)
        }
        ExprKind::App { fun, args } => {
            let callee_ok = match &fun.kind {
                ExprKind::Var(n) => local_scc.contains(n) || pure.contains(n),
                ExprKind::Fn { body, .. } => wpo_pure_expr(body, pure, local_scc),
                _ => wpo_pure_expr(fun, pure, local_scc),
            };
            callee_ok && args.iter().all(|a| wpo_pure_expr(a, pure, local_scc))
        }
        ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } => {
            wpo_pure_expr(scrut, pure, local_scc)
                && arms.iter().all(|a| wpo_pure_expr(&a.body, pure, local_scc))
        }
        ExprKind::Block { stmts, tail } => {
            stmts.iter().all(|s| wpo_pure_expr(s, pure, local_scc))
                && wpo_pure_expr(tail, pure, local_scc)
        }
        ExprKind::Bin { l, r, .. } => {
            wpo_pure_expr(l, pure, local_scc) && wpo_pure_expr(r, pure, local_scc)
        }
        ExprKind::Un { e, .. } => wpo_pure_expr(e, pure, local_scc),
    }
}

fn wpo_collect_pure(p: &Program, graph: &HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>) -> HashSet<String> {
    let mut pure = HashSet::<String>::new();

    for d in &p.decls {
        match d {
            Decl::External(e) if e.pure => {
                pure.insert(e.name.clone());
            }
            Decl::Type { ctors, .. } => {
                for c in ctors {
                    pure.insert(c.name.clone());
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    let sccs = wpo_sccs(graph);
    loop {
        let mut changed = false;
        for comp in &sccs {
            let local: HashSet<String> = comp.iter().cloned().collect();
            let mut ok = true;
            for d in &p.decls {
                let Decl::Let { name, value, .. } = d else {
                    continue;
                };
                if !local.contains(name) {
                    continue;
                }
                let ExprKind::Fn { body, .. } = &value.kind else {
                    ok = false;
                    break;
                };
                if !wpo_pure_expr(body, &pure, &local) {
                    ok = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if ok {
                for n in comp {
                    changed |= pure.insert(n.clone());
                }
            }
        }
        if !changed {
            break;
        }
    }
    pure
}

fn wpo_summary(p: &Program) -> WpoSummary {
    let functions = wpo_function_map(p);
    let (graph, calls) = wpo_graph_and_calls(p, &functions);
    let recursive = wpo_recursive_from_graph(&graph);
    let pure = wpo_collect_pure(p, &graph);
    let sizes = functions
        .iter()
        .map(|(n, (_, body))| (n.clone(), size(body)))
        .collect();
    WpoSummary {
        functions,
        graph,
        recursive,
        pure,
        calls,
        sizes,
    }
}

fn wpo_pat_int(p: &Pat, value: u64, binds: &mut Vec<(String, u64)>) -> bool {
    match p {
        Pat::Wild => true,
        Pat::Bind(n) => {
            binds.push((n.clone(), value));
            true
        }
        Pat::Int(n) => *n == value,
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn wpo_eval_int(
    e: &Expr,
    env: &mut Vec<(String, u64)>,
    funcs: &HashMap<String, (Vec<String>, Expr)>,
    pure: &HashSet<String>,
    fuel: &mut usize,
) -> Option<u64> {
    if *fuel == 0 {
        return None;
    }
    *fuel -= 1;
    match &e.kind {
        ExprKind::Int(n) => Some(*n),
        ExprKind::Var(n) => env.iter().rev().find(|(k, _)| k == n).map(|(_, v)| *v),
        ExprKind::Un { op, e } => {
            wpo_eval_int(e, env, funcs, pure, fuel).and_then(|v| eval_un(*op, v))
        }
        ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } => {
            let a = wpo_eval_int(l, env, funcs, pure, fuel)?;
            let b = wpo_eval_int(r, env, funcs, pure, fuel)?;
            eval_bin(*op, a, b)
        }
        ExprKind::Let { name, value, body } => {
            let v = wpo_eval_int(value, env, funcs, pure, fuel)?;
            env.push((name.clone(), v));
            let out = wpo_eval_int(body, env, funcs, pure, fuel);
            env.pop();
            out
        }
        ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } => {
            let v = wpo_eval_int(scrut, env, funcs, pure, fuel)?;
            for arm in arms {
                let mut binds = Vec::new();
                if wpo_pat_int(&arm.pat, v, &mut binds) {
                    let old = env.len();
                    for b in binds {
                        env.push(b);
                    }
                    let out = wpo_eval_int(&arm.body, env, funcs, pure, fuel);
                    env.truncate(old);
                    return out;
                }
            }
            None
        }
        ExprKind::Block { stmts, tail } => {
            for s in stmts {
                let _ = wpo_eval_int(s, env, funcs, pure, fuel);
            }
            wpo_eval_int(tail, env, funcs, pure, fuel)
        }
        ExprKind::App { fun, args } => {
            let ExprKind::Var(name) = &fun.kind else {
                return None;
            };
            if !pure.contains(name) {
                return None;
            }
            let (params, body) = funcs.get(name)?;
            if params.len() != args.len() {
                return None;
            }
            let mut values = Vec::with_capacity(args.len());
            for a in args {
                values.push(wpo_eval_int(a, env, funcs, pure, fuel)?);
            }
            let old = env.len();
            for (param, value) in params.iter().zip(values) {
                env.push((param.clone(), value));
            }
            let out = wpo_eval_int(body, env, funcs, pure, fuel);
            env.truncate(old);
            out
        }
        ExprKind::Fn { .. } | ExprKind::Float(_) | ExprKind::Str(_) | ExprKind::Quote(_) => None,
    }
}

fn wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(
    e: Expr,
    summary: &WpoSummary,
    changed: &mut bool,
    folds: &mut usize,
) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let e = match e.kind {
        ExprKind::Let { name, value, body } => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: ExprKind::Let {
                name,
                value: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*value, summary, changed, folds)),
                body: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*body, summary, changed, folds)),
            },
        },
        ExprKind::Fn { params, body } => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: ExprKind::Fn {
                params,
                body: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*body, summary, changed, folds)),
            },
        },
        ExprKind::App { fun, args } => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: ExprKind::App {
                fun: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*fun, summary, changed, folds)),
                args: args
                    .into_iter()
                    .map(|a| wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(a, summary, changed, folds))
                    .collect(),
            },
        },
        ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: ExprKind::Case {
                scrut: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*scrut, summary, changed, folds)),
                arms: arms
                    .into_iter()
                    .map(|a| Arm {
                        pat: a.pat,
                        body: wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(a.body, summary, changed, folds),
                        span: a.span,
                    })
                    .collect(),
            },
        },
        ExprKind::Block { stmts, tail } => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: ExprKind::Block {
                stmts: stmts
                    .into_iter()
                    .map(|s| wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(s, summary, changed, folds))
                    .collect(),
                tail: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*tail, summary, changed, folds)),
            },
        },
        ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: ExprKind::Bin {
                op,
                l: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*l, summary, changed, folds)),
                r: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*r, summary, changed, folds)),
            },
        },
        ExprKind::Un { op, e } => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                op,
                e: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*e, summary, changed, folds)),
            },
        },
        ExprKind::Quote(x) => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: ExprKind::Quote(Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(
                *x, summary, changed, folds,
            ))),
        },
        other => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: other,
        },
    };

    if let ExprKind::App { fun, args } = &e.kind {
        if let ExprKind::Var(name) = &fun.kind {
            if summary.pure.contains(name)
                && summary.functions.contains_key(name)
                && args.iter().all(|a| matches!(a.kind, ExprKind::Int(_)))
                && args.len() <= 12
            {
                let mut env = Vec::new();
                let mut fuel = 4096usize;
                if let Some(v) =
                    wpo_eval_int(&e, &mut env, &summary.functions, &summary.pure, &mut fuel)
                {
                    *changed = true;
                    *folds += 1;
                    return Expr {
                        span: sp,
                        kind: ExprKind::Int(v),
                    };
                }
            }
        }
    }
    e
}

fn wpo_fold_word_calls(
    mut p: Program,
    summary: &WpoSummary,
    changed: &mut bool,
    folds: &mut usize,
) -> Program {
    p.decls = p
        .decls
        .into_iter()
        .map(|d| match d {
            Decl::Let { name, value, span } => Decl::Let {
                name,
                value: Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*value, summary, changed, folds)),
                span,
            },
            other => other,
        })
        .collect();
    p.body = Box::new(wpo_fold_word_calls_expr(*p.body, summary, changed, folds));
    p
}

fn wpo_adaptive_inline(p: Program, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Program {
    let summary = wpo_summary(&p);
    if summary.functions.is_empty() {
        return p;
    }
    let mut used = identifiers(&p);
    let mut serial = 0usize;

    fn inline_one(
        params: &[String],
        body: &Expr,
        args: &[Expr],
        pure: &HashSet<String>,
        used: &mut HashSet<String>,
        serial: &mut usize,
    ) -> Option<Expr> {
        if params.len() != args.len() || !distinct(params) {
            return None;
        }
        let mut b = body.clone();
        let mut lets = Vec::<(String, Expr)>::new();
        for (param, arg) in params.iter().zip(args) {
            let uses = count_var(&b, param);
            if uses == 0 {
                if is_pure(arg, pure) {
                    continue;
                }
            }
            if uses <= 1 || is_pure(arg, pure) {
                b = subst(b, param, arg);
            } else {
                let fresh = fresh_opt_name(used, serial, "ip");
                b = subst(
                    b,
                    param,
                    &Expr {
                        span: arg.span,
                        kind: ExprKind::Var(fresh.clone()),
                    },
                );
                lets.push((fresh, arg.clone()));
            }
            if count_var(&b, param) != 0 {
                return None;
            }
        }
        for (name, value) in lets.into_iter().rev() {
            b = Expr {
                span: b.span,
                kind: ExprKind::Let {
                    name,
                    value: Box::new(value),
                    body: Box::new(b),
                },
            };
        }
        Some(b)
    }

    fn go(
        e: Expr,
        summary: &WpoSummary,
        pure: &HashSet<String>,
        changed: &mut bool,
        used: &mut HashSet<String>,
        serial: &mut usize,
    ) -> Expr {
        let sp = e.span;
        let e = match e.kind {
            ExprKind::Let { name, value, body } => Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Let {
                    name,
                    value: Box::new(go(*value, summary, pure, changed, used, serial)),
                    body: Box::new(go(*body, summary, pure, changed, used, serial)),
                },
            },
            ExprKind::Fn { params, body } => Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Fn {
                    params,
                    body: Box::new(go(*body, summary, pure, changed, used, serial)),
                },
            },
            ExprKind::App { fun, args } => Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::App {
                    fun: Box::new(go(*fun, summary, pure, changed, used, serial)),
                    args: args
                        .into_iter()
                        .map(|a| go(a, summary, pure, changed, used, serial))
                        .collect(),
                },
            },
            ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } => Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Case {
                    scrut: Box::new(go(*scrut, summary, pure, changed, used, serial)),
                    arms: arms
                        .into_iter()
                        .map(|a| Arm {
                            pat: a.pat,
                            body: go(a.body, summary, pure, changed, used, serial),
                            span: a.span,
                        })
                        .collect(),
                },
            },
            ExprKind::Block { stmts, tail } => Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Block {
                    stmts: stmts
                        .into_iter()
                        .map(|x| go(x, summary, pure, changed, used, serial))
                        .collect(),
                    tail: Box::new(go(*tail, summary, pure, changed, used, serial)),
                },
            },
            ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } => Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Bin {
                    op,
                    l: Box::new(go(*l, summary, pure, changed, used, serial)),
                    r: Box::new(go(*r, summary, pure, changed, used, serial)),
                },
            },
            ExprKind::Un { op, e } => Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Un {
                    op,
                    e: Box::new(go(*e, summary, pure, changed, used, serial)),
                },
            },
            ExprKind::Quote(x) => Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Quote(Box::new(go(*x, summary, pure, changed, used, serial))),
            },
            other => Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: other,
            },
        };

        if let ExprKind::App { fun, args } = &e.kind {
            if let ExprKind::Var(name) = &fun.kind {
                if let Some((params, body)) = summary.functions.get(name) {
                    if !summary.recursive.contains(name)
                        && params.len() == args.len()
                        && !params.is_empty()
                        && size(body) <= 384
                    {
                        let uses = *summary.calls.get(name).unwrap_or(&1);
                        let limit = if uses <= 1 {
                            384
                        } else if pure.contains(name) {
                            192
                        } else {
                            96
                        };
                        if size(body) <= limit {
                            let constant_arg = args.iter().any(|a| {
                                matches!(
                                    a.kind,
                                    ExprKind::Int(_) | ExprKind::Float(_) | ExprKind::Str(_)
                                )
                            });
                            let hot_or_const = uses <= 1 || constant_arg || pure.contains(name);
                            if hot_or_const {
                                if let Some(out) =
                                    inline_one(params, body, args, pure, used, serial)
                                {
                                    *changed = true;
                                    return out;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        e
    }

    let mut out = p;
    out.decls = out
        .decls
        .into_iter()
        .map(|d| match d {
            Decl::Let { name, value, span } => Decl::Let {
                name,
                value: Box::new(go(*value, &summary, pure, changed, &mut used, &mut serial)),
                span,
            },
            other => other,
        })
        .collect();
    out.body = Box::new(go(
        *out.body,
        &summary,
        pure,
        changed,
        &mut used,
        &mut serial,
    ));
    out
}

fn wpo_cost(p: &Program) -> usize {
    let m = measure(p);
    m.nodes
        .saturating_add(m.binders.saturating_mul(3))
        .saturating_add(m.fns.saturating_mul(5))
}

#[inline]
fn opt_cost(p: &Program) -> usize {
    let m = measure(p);
    m.nodes
        .saturating_add(m.binders.saturating_mul(3))
        .saturating_add(m.fns.saturating_mul(5))
}

fn wpo_commit<F>(
    p: Program,
    pure: &HashSet<String>,
    label: &str,
    stats: &mut WpoStats,
    f: F,
) -> (Program, bool)
where
    F: FnOnce(Program, &HashSet<String>, &mut bool) -> Program,
{
    let before_hash = fingerprint(&p);
    let before_cost = wpo_cost(&p);
    let before_measure = measure(&p);
    let mut changed = false;

    opt_debug!(
        "[WPO] pass={} BEGIN: hash={:016x}, nodes={}, fns={}, binders={}, cost={}",
        label,
        before_hash,
        before_measure.nodes,
        before_measure.fns,
        before_measure.binders,
        before_cost
    );

    let candidate = f(p.clone(), pure, &mut changed);
    let after_hash = fingerprint(&candidate);

    if !changed {
        opt_debug!(
            "[WPO] pass={} KEEP: transform reported changed=false; candidate discarded without AST checks",
            label
        );
        return (p, false);
    }

    opt_debug_delta!(&format!("[WPO] {}", label), &p, &candidate);

    if after_hash == before_hash {
        stats.rejected += 1;
        opt_debug!(
            "[WPO] pass={} REVERT: transform claimed a rewrite, but structural fingerprint stayed {:016x}; reason=NO_STRUCTURAL_CHANGE",
            label,
            before_hash
        );
        return (p, false);
    }

    let _ = (before_cost, wpo_cost(&candidate));

    let scope_ok = scoped(&candidate);
    if !scope_ok {
        stats.rejected += 1;
        opt_debug!(
            "[WPO] pass={} REVERT: scope validation failed; reason=SCOPE_INVARIANT",
            label
        );
        return (p, false);
    }

    *stats.phase_hits.entry(label.to_string()).or_default() += 1;
    stats.commits += 1;
    opt_debug!(
        "[WPO] pass={} COMMIT: candidate accepted; commits={} rejects={}",
        label,
        stats.commits,
        stats.rejected
    );
    (candidate, true)
}

fn whole_program_optimize(mut p: Program) -> (Program, WpoStats) {
    let mut stats = WpoStats::default();
    let mut last = fingerprint(&p);

    for round in 0..WPO_ROUNDS {
        if stats.commits >= WPO_MAX_COMMITS {
            opt_debug!(
                "[WPO] STOP: commit limit reached ({} >= {})",
                stats.commits,
                WPO_MAX_COMMITS
            );
            break;
        }
        stats.rounds += 1;
        opt_debug!(
            "[WPO] ROUND {} BEGIN: hash={:016x}, nodes={}, fns={}, binders={}",
            round + 1,
            fingerprint(&p),
            measure(&p).nodes,
            measure(&p).fns,
            measure(&p).binders
        );
        let summary = wpo_summary(&p);
        let _ = (&summary.graph, &summary.calls, &summary.sizes);

        let before_round = fingerprint(&p);

        let mut folds = 0usize;
        let (next, ok) = wpo_commit(
            p,
            &summary.pure,
            "word-sccp",
            &mut stats,
            |p, _, changed| wpo_fold_word_calls(p, &summary, changed, &mut folds),
        );
        p = next;
        stats.const_folds += folds;
        if !ok && folds != 0 {
            stats.const_folds = stats.const_folds.saturating_sub(folds);
        }

        let phases: [(&str, fn(Program, &HashSet<String>, &mut bool) -> Program); 14] = [
            ("beta", beta),
            ("known-case", known_cases),
            ("instcombine", tree_opt),
            ("sccp", const_opt),
            ("global-sccp", global_const_opt),
            ("jump-thread", adv_opt),
            ("gvn-cse", adv_opt),
            ("arg-dce", drop_params),
            ("sink-sroa", sink),
            ("mega-local", mega_opt),
            ("case-fold", flatten_cases),
            ("closure-strip", strip_closure_surface),
            ("adce", dce_changed),
            ("cleanup", tree_opt),
        ];

        for (label, pass) in phases {
            if stats.commits >= WPO_MAX_COMMITS {
                break;
            }
            let summary = wpo_summary(&p);
            let before = fingerprint(&p);
            let (next, ok) = wpo_commit(p, &summary.pure, label, &mut stats, pass);
            p = next;
            if label == "gvn-cse" {
                let summary = wpo_summary(&p);
                let (next, _) = wpo_commit(
                    p,
                    &summary.pure,
                    "partial-inline",
                    &mut stats,
                    wpo_adaptive_inline,
                );
                p = next;
            }
            if ok && fingerprint(&p) != before {
                continue;
            }
        }

        let summary = wpo_summary(&p);
        let (next, _) = wpo_commit(p, &summary.pure, "whole-dce", &mut stats, dce_changed);
        p = next;

        let now = fingerprint(&p);
        if now == before_round || now == last {
            opt_debug!(
                "[WPO] ROUND {} END: fixed point reached; before={:016x}, after={:016x}, previous={:016x}",
                round + 1,
                before_round,
                now,
                last
            );
            break;
        }
        opt_debug!(
            "[WPO] ROUND {} END: state changed; {:016x} -> {:016x}; continuing whole-program analysis",
            round + 1,
            before_round,
            now
        );
        last = now;

        if round + 1 == WPO_ROUNDS {
            break;
        }
    }

    (p, stats)
}

fn print_wpo_stats(before: &Measure, after: &Measure, s: &WpoStats, p: &Program) {
    let summary = wpo_summary(p);
    let mut phase_parts = Vec::new();
    let order = [
        "word-sccp",
        "beta",
        "known-case",
        "instcombine",
        "sccp",
        "global-sccp",
        "jump-thread",
        "gvn-cse",
        "partial-inline",
        "arg-dce",
        "sink-sroa",
        "mega-local",
        "case-fold",
        "closure-strip",
        "adce",
        "cleanup",
        "whole-dce",
    ];
    for name in order {
        if let Some(n) = s.phase_hits.get(name) {
            if *n != 0 {
                phase_parts.push(format!("{}={}", name, n));
            }
        }
    }
    eprintln!(
        "  IPO: {} functions, {} recursive, {} pure, {} SCCs, {} call edges",
        summary.functions.len(),
        summary.recursive.len(),
        summary
            .pure
            .iter()
            .filter(|n| summary.functions.contains_key(*n))
            .count(),
        wpo_sccs(&summary.graph).len(),
        summary.graph.values().map(HashSet::len).sum::<usize>(),
    );
    eprintln!(
        "  rounds: {}  commits: {}  rejected: {}  word folds: {}",
        s.rounds, s.commits, s.rejected, s.const_folds
    );
    if phase_parts.is_empty() {
        eprintln!("  passes: no effective WPO rewrites");
    } else {
        eprintln!("  passes: {}", phase_parts.join("  "));
    }
    let node_delta = after.nodes as isize - before.nodes as isize;
    if node_delta <= 0 {
        eprintln!(
            "  AST: {} -> {} nodes (-{})",
            before.nodes, after.nodes, -node_delta
        );
    } else {
        eprintln!(
            "  AST: {} -> {} nodes (+{})",
            before.nodes, after.nodes, node_delta
        );
    }
}

pub fn run(p: Program) -> Program {
    let start = measure(&p);
    eprintln!("→ optimize: normalize");
    let p_original = p.clone();
    let p = alpha(p);
    let norm = measure(&p);
    let norm_decls = p.decls.len();
    eprintln!(
        "  normalized: {} nodes, {} functions, {} binders",
        norm.nodes, norm.fns, norm.binders
    );

    if !scoped(&p) {
        eprintln!("✗ optimize: initial scope validation failed; original program kept");
        eprintln!("  [run-debug] returning the PRE-alpha program (the alpha'd one is broken)");
        return p_original;
    }

    eprintln!("→ optimize: analyze");
    let analysis = wpo_summary(&p);
    let pure_fns = analysis
        .pure
        .iter()
        .filter(|name| analysis.functions.contains_key(*name))
        .count();
    eprintln!(
        "  whole-program: {} functions, {} SCCs, {} recursive, {} pure, {} call edges",
        analysis.functions.len(),
        wpo_sccs(&analysis.graph).len(),
        analysis.recursive.len(),
        pure_fns,
        analysis.graph.values().map(HashSet::len).sum::<usize>(),
    );

    eprintln!("→ optimize: whole-program / LLVM-class AST");
    let wpo_before = measure(&p);
    let (wpo_p, wpo_stats) = whole_program_optimize(p);
    let wpo_after = measure(&wpo_p);
    print_wpo_stats(&wpo_before, &wpo_after, &wpo_stats, &wpo_p);
    let mut p = wpo_p;
    let mut seen = HashSet::<u64>::new();
    let mut stats = OptStats::default();
    let mut quarantined = HashSet::<String>::new();
    let mut nodes = wpo_after.nodes;
    let mut state = fingerprint(&p);
    seen.insert(state);
    let pass_count = 15usize;

    eprintln!("→ optimize: simplify");
    eprintln!(
        "  pipeline: {} global layers, {} local rewrite layers",
        pass_count, LOCAL_LAYERS
    );
    loop {
        stats.rounds += 1;
        opt_debug!(
            "[LOCAL] SWEEP {} BEGIN: hash={:016x}, nodes={}, fns={}, binders={}",
            stats.rounds,
            fingerprint(&p),
            measure(&p).nodes,
            measure(&p).fns,
            measure(&p).binders
        );
        let summary = wpo_summary(&p);
        let pure = summary.pure;
        let mut restarted = false;

        for pass in 0..pass_count {
            let (label, protect_rec) = match pass {
                0 => ("beta", false),
                1 => ("known-case", false),
                2 => ("tree", false),
                3 => ("const", false),
                4 => ("global-const", false),
                5 => ("param-drop", true),
                6 => ("inline", false),
                7 => ("mega", false),
                8 => ("advanced", false),
                9 => ("sink", false),
                10 => ("dce", false),
                11 => ("global-sccp", false),
                12 => ("jump-thread", false),
                13 => ("word-sccp", false),
                14 => ("whole-dce", false),
                _ => unreachable!(),
            };

            if quarantined.contains(label) {
                opt_debug!(
                    "[LOCAL] pass={} SKIP: already quarantined after a prior failed validation",
                    label
                );
                continue;
            }

            let (candidate, accepted, next_state, next_nodes) = match label {
                "beta" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    beta,
                ),
                "known-case" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    known_cases,
                ),
                "tree" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    tree_opt,
                ),
                "const" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    const_opt,
                ),
                "global-const" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    global_const_opt,
                ),
                "param-drop" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    drop_params,
                ),
                "inline" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    inline,
                ),
                "mega" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    mega_opt,
                ),
                "advanced" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    adv_opt,
                ),
                "sink" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    sink,
                ),
                "dce" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    dce_changed,
                ),
                "global-sccp" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    global_const_opt,
                ),
                "jump-thread" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    adv_opt,
                ),
                "word-sccp" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    const_opt,
                ),
                "whole-dce" => guard_pass(
                    p,
                    &pure,
                    &mut stats,
                    label,
                    protect_rec,
                    state,
                    nodes,
                    &seen,
                    &mut quarantined,
                    dce_changed,
                ),
                _ => unreachable!(),
            };

            p = candidate;
            if accepted {
                opt_debug!(
                    "[LOCAL] pass={} COMMIT: restart optimizer from new state hash={:016x}, nodes={} -> {}; accepted_restarts={}",
                    label,
                    next_state,
                    nodes,
                    next_nodes,
                    stats.restarts + 1
                );
                state = next_state;
                nodes = next_nodes;
                stats.restarts += 1;
                restarted = true;
                break;
            } else {
                opt_debug!(
                    "[LOCAL] pass={} KEEP: original state preserved; next pass may still examine it",
                    label
                );
            }
        }

        if !restarted {
            opt_debug!(
                "[LOCAL] SWEEP {} END: no pass committed; fixed point at hash={:016x}",
                stats.rounds,
                fingerprint(&p)
            );
            break;
        }
        opt_debug!(
            "[LOCAL] SWEEP {} END: committed pass forced restart; new hash={:016x}",
            stats.rounds,
            fingerprint(&p)
        );
    }

    stats.quarantined = quarantined;
    eprintln!("  sweeps: {}", stats.rounds);
    eprintln!("  restarts: {}", stats.restarts);
    eprintln!("  fixed point: no further rewrites");
    print_pass_stats(&stats);

    eprintln!("→ optimize: finalize");

    let p = match crate::sema::analyze(p.clone()) {
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(errs) => {
            for e in &errs {
                eprintln!(
                    "  [opt-final] semantic invariant: {} (span {}..{})",
                    e.msg, e.span.lo, e.span.hi
                );
            }
            return p;
        }
    };
    let final_ok = scoped(&p);
    let end = measure(&p);
    opt_debug!(
        "[VERIFY] final scope={} hash={:016x} nodes={} fns={} binders={} decls={}",
        final_ok,
        fingerprint(&p),
        end.nodes,
        end.fns,
        end.binders,
        p.decls.len()
    );
    if final_ok {
        let delta = end.nodes as isize - norm.nodes as isize;
        let pct = pct_delta(norm.nodes, end.nodes).abs();
        if delta <= 0 {
            eprintln!(
                "✓ optimize: reduced {} -> {} nodes (-{} / {:.1}%), {} -> {} functions, {} -> {} declarations",
                norm.nodes,
                end.nodes,
                -delta,
                pct,
                norm.fns,
                end.fns,
                norm_decls,
                p.decls.len()
            );
        } else {
            eprintln!(
                "✓ optimize: grew {} -> {} nodes (+{} / {:.1}%), {} -> {} functions, {} -> {} declarations",
                norm.nodes,
                end.nodes,
                delta,
                pct,
                norm.fns,
                end.fns,
                norm_decls,
                p.decls.len()
            );
        }
        if start.nodes != norm.nodes {
            eprintln!("  normalization: {} -> {} nodes", start.nodes, norm.nodes);
        }
    } else {
        eprintln!("✗ optimize: final scope invariant failed; returning current AST");
    }
    p
}

const HASH_OFFSET: u64 = 0xcbf29ce484222325;
const HASH_PRIME: u64 = 0x100000001b3;

#[inline(always)]
fn hash_byte(h: &mut u64, b: u8) {
